# Triage report: opencv/opencv

2951 open items (2571 issues, about 2445 distinct problems). Proposals only: nothing here changes GitHub.

## How this report is made

1. **Crawl repo.** Every issue and PR of the repository, open and closed, is copied with its comments into a local database, together with the links GitHub itself keeps (which PR closes which issue, which issue a maintainer marked as a duplicate). Code is never read.
2. **Read and trim.** Each item becomes one vector from its title, its description and a short digest of its comments, made by a small local language-embedding model. Items about the same thing end up close to each other.
3. **Compare and group.** Pairs that are very close are proposed as duplicates, as an old bug coming back, or as an issue and the PR that fixed it without saying so. GitHub's own links are used as they are.
4. **Suggest label.** A missing label comes from the title ("BUG:", "f2py") or from the labels of the most similar labeled items.
5. **Score and check.** Each proposal gets the algorithm's score; person and LLM recheck afterwards.

## Tech used

- **Open issues already marked as duplicates**: GitHub GraphQL `MarkedAsDuplicateEvent`: a maintainer's own action.
- **Open issues whose closing PR is merged**: GitHub GraphQL `closingIssuesReferences` and "fixes #N" in the PR's description, PR merged (high confidence means the link is most likely true, neither the issue nor the PR is done).
- **Likely duplicates of an older open issue**: bge-small-en embeddings (title + description, averaged with an extractive MMR digest of the comments); high similarity between two open issues (confidence = similarity).
- **Open issues very similar to an older closed one (came back, or a duplicate of something settled?)**: bge-small-en embeddings (title + description, averaged with an extractive MMR digest of the comments); high similarity to an older closed issue (confidence = similarity).
- **Open issues that a later merged PR seems to fix**: bge-small-en embeddings (title + description, averaged with an extractive MMR digest of the comments); the issue and a later merged PR are each other's nearest neighbor, high similarity, no closing link (confidence = similarity).
- **Open PRs after the same thing**: GitHub closing links to the same issue, or bge-small-en embeddings (title + description, averaged with an extractive MMR digest of the comments) with high similarity between open PRs of different authors (confidence = similarity).
- **Labels to add**: title rules learned from labeled items (prefix such as BUG:/ENH:, a component's name; confidence = the rule's hit rate); type: kNN over the nearest labeled items (confidence = weighted share carrying the label); component: votes of the nearest labeled items plus linked items (confidence = share of the votes).

<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/8052">#8052</a> Cannot create non uniform histogram using cv2.calcHist()  in Python 2.7</td><td>duplicate of the older open #4502 (0.91)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues report that cv2.calcHist() Python bindings do not support non-uniform histograms with custom bin edges, showing the same core limitation.</td></tr>

<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23826">#23826</a> CMake Error at /usr/local/share/cmake-3.26/Modules/FindCUDA.cmake:1767 (file)</td><td>duplicate of the older open #23212 (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve CUDA/CMake integration issues in OpenCV but differ: A is a generator expression error during OpenCV build, B is deprecated FindCUDA usage in OpenCVConfig.cmake for consumer projects.</td></tr>

<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/18654">#18654</a> Can not build macOS framework</td><td>like #13759, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve macOS framework build failures in build_framework.py, but A fails on opencv-onnx.pb.cc (dnn module) while B fails on stereobm.cpp (calib3d) with SSE4.1 errors; different root causes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/19366">#19366</a> Unsupported data type: BOOL in function &#x27;getMatFromTensor&#x27; after call readNetFromONNX (TensorFlow -&gt; ONNX)</td><td>like #15129, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve unsupported data types in OpenCV&#x27;s DNN module when loading models, but they occur in different parsers (ONNX vs TensorFlow) and different source files (onnx_graph_simplifier.cpp vs tf_graph_simplifier.cpp).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/20997">#20997</a> PyCharm cannot find cv2 references with the most recent version</td><td>like #18728, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues describe PyCharm&#x27;s inability to resolve cv2 references due to the module&#x27;s binary extension structure and dynamic loading in __init__.py, with identical symptoms and root cause.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/21814">#21814</a> undefined references in `libopencv_core.so` (3.2 and 4.2)</td><td>like #21810, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues report missing implementations of SparseMatConstIterator decrement operators (prefix in A, postfix in B) in the same source file, with a maintainer explicitly noting B as a possible duplicate of A.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22011">#22011</a> In Galaxy S22 and Android Version12, VideoCapture::bool read Crash erupt</td><td>like #21149, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues are VideoCapture crashes on Android 12 Samsung devices caused by MediaCodec decoder reporting frame dimensions (crop height) that don&#x27;t match the actual allocated buffer size, leading to SIGSEGV when copying frame data; Issue A explicitly references #21149 as similar and the root cause is identical.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22152">#22152</a> SIFT，detectAndCompute，Memory leak</td><td>like #21080, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve memory issues in SIFT detectAndCompute, but A shows gradual leak in a loop while B shows excessive allocation on a specific high-res image.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22157">#22157</a> ModuleNotFoundError: No module named &#x27;cv2&#x27;</td><td>like #7045, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve &#x27;No module named cv2&#x27; on Ubuntu after building OpenCV, but A is a Python package path misconfiguration (site-packages vs dist-packages) in 4.6.0, while B is missing Python3 dev dependencies preventing bindings build in 3.x.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22590">#22590</a> Failed to build iOS framework</td><td>like #22487, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve iOS framework build failures but with different root causes (objc bindings compilation error vs Python script bytes/string regex issue).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22658">#22658</a> Incorrect size of input array (Inconsistent shape for ConcatLayer)</td><td>like #10739, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve &#x27;Inconsistent shape for ConcatLayer&#x27; errors in the DNN module, but A appears to be a user input size mismatch with an ONNX model while B was a confirmed slice layer bug in TensorFlow import fixed by PR #10748.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22881">#22881</a> Reading images with german &quot;Umlaute&quot; (ä, ö, ü) in path</td><td>like #17107, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues describe cv2.imread failing to read images when the file path contains German Umlaute (ä, ö, ü, Ä, Ö, Ü) on Windows.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23252">#23252</a> Memory leak with std::async</td><td>like #9745, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues describe the same memory leak caused by OpenCV&#x27;s TLS data not being released when threads created via std::async terminate, with issue A explicitly referencing issue B.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23298">#23298</a> OpenCV cannot import ONNX model: Can&#x27;t create layer &quot;onnx_node!NonMaxSuppression_683&quot; of type &quot;NonMaxSuppression&quot; in function &#x27;cv::dnn::dnn4_v20221220::Net::Impl::getLayerInstance&#x27;</td><td>like #22631, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are ONNX model import failures in OpenCV DNN module, but A lacks NonMaxSuppression operator support while B fails due to duplicate tensor names causing shape assertion.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23428">#23428</a> Windows10 OpenCV4.7 WITH_CUDA=ON BuildError opencv_core</td><td>like #19888, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve CUDA build failures on Windows when compiling gpu_mat.cu in the core module, but with different OpenCV/CUDA/VS versions and error codes (MSB8066 vs MSB6006), suggesting similar symptom area but likely different root causes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23430">#23430</a> I can&#x27;t set the buffersize in opencv </td><td>like #10671, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve inability to set CAP_PROP_BUFFERSIZE, but A concerns FFmpeg/GStreamer backend for RTMP network streams while B concerns V4L2 backend for local cameras.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23507">#23507</a> Unable to build macOS Catalyst version of OpenCV framework (osx/build_frameworks.py)</td><td>like #17291, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues concern Mac Catalyst support in OpenCV&#x27;s build scripts, but A is a specific build failure due to outdated compiler target (ios13.0-macabi) while B is a broader feature request for xcframework support to resolve architecture conflicts.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23593">#23593</a> Build iOS Framework Error</td><td>like #12646, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve iOS framework build failures via build_framework.py but differ in error phase (CMake configure vs xcodebuild compile), OpenCV versions, and root causes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/24110">#24110</a> OpenCV4.8.0 dnn can not load fp16 onnx model which converts from onnxmltools</td><td>like #18735, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve fp16 ONNX models from onnxmltools failing in OpenCV DNN, but B was a missing FP16 support feature (fixed in #22337) while A is a new parsing bug with Resize nodes in a version that should support FP16.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/24430">#24430</a> Your platform doesn&#x27;t suppport hardware accelerated AV1 decoding. Failed to get pixel format.</td><td>like #22304, closed as not planned (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues report the identical AV1 decoding error caused by OpenCV&#x27;s bundled GPL-free FFmpeg lacking libdav1d support, just on different platforms (Linux vs Windows).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/24506">#24506</a> windows7 install opencv with cuda error</td><td>like #20863, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve building OpenCV with CUDA on Windows and enabling OPENCV_DNN_CUDA, but A is a build failure due to CUDA 10.2 not supporting RTX 3090 (compute capability 8.6) while B was a CMake configuration error from not enabling WITH_CUDA.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/25929">#25929</a> Built target opencv_cudafilters make error</td><td>like #18543, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve build failures at the opencv_cudafilters stage with CUDA, but A shows specific TIFF linker errors due to conda conflict while B suggests a parallel build issue with no detailed error.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/26250">#26250</a> Windows get camera cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS incorrect value for low fps.</td><td>like #24000, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve incorrect FPS reporting on Windows but differ in scenario (live camera setting vs video file reading) and likely code paths (cap_dshow.cpp vs MSMF/FFMPEG backend).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/27200">#27200</a> Enhance OpenCV DNN for LLM Inference: Dynamic Memory, KV Caching, and Dynamic Sequence Extension</td><td>like #27159, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues propose identical enhancements for LLM inference in OpenCV DNN (dynamic memory allocation, KV caching, PagedAttention) referencing the same GSoC 2025 idea; Issue B is the completed implementation plan for what Issue A proposes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29238">#29238</a> Misaligned Memory Read in `cv::resize()` via SIMD `v_lut_pairs()` with Odd Byte Offset</td><td>like #28597, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues report the exact same misaligned memory access bug in v_lut_pairs() at intrin_sse.hpp:3080 triggered by cv::resize() with odd byte offsets.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29419">#29419</a> opencv 5 is unbuildable except with a particular old version of protobuf</td><td>like #23791, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve protobuf compatibility problems in OpenCV&#x27;s dnn module related to caffe protobuf files, but they affect different versions (5 vs 4.7) with distinct root causes: stale generated files after Caffe removal vs C++ standard incompatibility with protobuf 3.x.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/30459">#30459</a> 5.x: warpAffine INTER_NEAREST picks different pixels depending on the image type</td><td>like #29279, closed as completed (0.92)</td><td><b>related</b>, Issue A describes a broader 5.x inconsistency in warpAffine INTER_NEAREST across data types (new kernels vs old invoker), which includes the IPP mismatch from Issue B (fixed in 4.x via #29554 but not yet merged to 5.x).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/5060">#5060</a> Error building OpenCV 3.0.0 on MacOS X</td><td>like #4518, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are CUDA build failures on macOS caused by compiler detection issues with clang, but they show different error symptoms (missing string.h vs unsupported -dumpspecs option) across different OpenCV versions.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/11277">#11277</a> Fails to compile &quot;hal_internal.cpp&quot;</td><td>like #6749, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are compilation errors in hal_internal.cpp involving complex number handling in BLAS/LAPACK gemm calls, but B was fixed in 2016 while A appears later as a similar issue in the same code area.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/18041">#18041</a> Makefile:160: recipe for target &#x27;all&#x27; failed</td><td>like #7652, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are OpenCV CUDA build failures but with different root causes: Issue A shows specific GCC intrinsic errors (__builtin_ia32_monitorx/mwaitx) on OpenCV 4.2.0, while Issue B is a generic make failure on OpenCV 3.1 linked to CUDA 8.0 API incompatibility.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/18183">#18183</a> error: (-2:Unspecified error) Input layer not found: functional_1/lstm_1/zeros_1/packed/1 in function &#x27;cv::dnn::dnn4_v20200609::`anonymous-namespace&#x27;::TFImporter::connect&#x27;</td><td>like #14527, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues show &#x27;Input layer not found&#x27; errors in TensorFlow importer when loading frozen graphs, but involve different unsupported layers (LSTM vs ROI pooling) and model architectures.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/18759">#18759</a> Getting the error when loading the .pb file using cv2.dnn.readNetFromTensorflow</td><td>like #11452, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, both readNetFromTensorflow failing on a .pb; custom layer vs const node assertion. <b>unrelated</b>, Different assertion errors in different functions (populateNet vs addConstNodes) with different root causes: Issue A involves unsupported TF ops (Range, Tile) causing CV_32FC1 type failure, while Issue B was a const_layers insertion assertion with various unrelated causes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/19737">#19737</a> Possible memory leak with MSMF backend for cv::videoCapture</td><td>like #13255, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve MSMF backend memory leaks in VideoCapture on Windows, but A is triggered by hardware acceleration during normal capture/write, while B is triggered by repeated open/close cycles.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/20907">#20907</a> Input layer not found in function &#x27;cv::dnn::dnn4_v20210608::`anonymous-namespace&#x27;::TFImporter::connect&#x27;</td><td>like #14527, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are &#x27;Input layer not found&#x27; errors in TFImporter::connect when loading custom TensorFlow frozen graphs, but involve different unsupported ops (Conv2DTranspose vs ROI/split).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/21396">#21396</a> python: opencv 4.5.3 ,cv2.error: OpenCV(4.5.3) C:\Users\runneradmin\AppData\Local\Temp\pip-req-build-q3d_8t8e\opencv\modules\dnn\src\model.cpp:1216: error: (-215:Assertion failed) length &gt; FLT_EPSILON in function &#x27;cv::dnn::TextDetectionModel_DB_Impl::unclip&#x27;</td><td>like #21395, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues report the identical assertion failure in TextDetectionModel_DB_Impl::unclip at model.cpp:1216 when using the model in concurrent contexts (Python threading, Java Singleton), confirming the same thread-safety bug.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/21419">#21419</a> OpenCV4.5.5 compilation error: [modules/python3/CMakeFiles/opencv_python3.dir/all] Error 2</td><td>like #16397, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, both building OpenCV with CUDA fails; different targets and versions. <b>unrelated</b>, Issue A is a Python bindings compilation error in GAPI module (GInferOutputs, GCompiloid), while Issue B is a missing header in samples/gpu (xfeatures2d/cuda.hpp); different modules, errors, and OpenCV versions.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/21571">#21571</a> OpenCV2 and iOS - Cannot build </td><td>like #12646, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve iOS framework build failures but with different root causes (arm64 simulator support vs CMake/Xcode 10 compatibility).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/22984">#22984</a> Build as iOS framework failed when using opencv_contrib module</td><td>like #6093, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, both build_framework.py for iOS fails; contrib modules vs zlib. <b>unrelated</b>, Issue A is a missing header (aruco_detector.hpp) when building with opencv_contrib, while Issue B is a zlib compilation error due to missing unistd.h on older iOS SDKs.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23097">#23097</a> VideoWriter not working with 4.7.0.68</td><td>like #16029, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve GStreamer backend issues with VideoWriter/VideoCapture but different symptoms (linking failure vs pipeline hang) and platforms.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23212">#23212</a> Opencv doesn&#x27;t compile with cmake 3.25</td><td>like #8625, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, both CMake configure with CUDA fails; FindCUDA removed in cmake 3.25 vs missing source file. <b>unrelated</b>, Issue A is about FindCUDA deprecation in CMake 3.25 when using OpenCV via find_package, while Issue B is about a stale CMake cache during OpenCV&#x27;s own build with CUDA in 2017.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23297">#23297</a> OpenCV cannot import ONNX model: inputs.size() &gt; 0 in function &#x27;cv::dnn::ReduceLayerImpl::getMemoryShapes&#x27;</td><td>like #16323, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve ONNX import failures in getMemoryShapes with inputs.size() assertion, but A is a ReduceLayer dynamic shape limitation while B appears to be a model export/layer-specific issue.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23396">#23396</a> CMake Error at cmake/OpenCVCompilerOptimizations.cmake:611 - Compiler doesn&#x27;t support baseline optimization flags</td><td>like #10363, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve iOS build failures with &#x27;Compiler doesn&#x27;t support baseline optimization flags&#x27; in OpenCVCompilerOptimizations.cmake, but Issue B (OpenCV 3.x, armv7/NEON) was fixed in 3.4.0 while Issue A (OpenCV 4.x, arm64/x86_64 simulator vs device) is a separate regression with newer toolchains.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/23548">#23548</a> Cant Build for iOS</td><td>like #12646, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both are iOS framework build failures using build_framework.py, but Issue A fails at CMake configuration with CheckTypeSize try_compile errors in libjpeg-turbo, while Issue B failed at compilation with a missing generated OpenCL kernel file, fixed by upgrading CMake.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/24831">#24831</a> ROI returned by cvGetOptimalNewCameraMatrix has wrong width &amp; height</td><td>like #7332, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, same function getOptimalNewCameraMatrix, wrong ROI vs odd result on own data. <b>unrelated</b>, Issue A is an off-by-one error in ROI calculation for zero-distortion case, while Issue B is about extreme distortion causing negative ROIs that clamp to zero.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/24971">#24971</a> findCirclesGrid can&#x27;t detect some asymmetric grids</td><td>like #24964, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both issues involve findCirclesGrid failing to detect grid patterns (asymmetric with perspective distortion vs rotated symmetric), and a proposed fix for A would also address B.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/25614">#25614</a> AttributeError: module &#x27;cv2.dnn&#x27; has no attribute &#x27;DictValue&#x27;</td><td>like #14706, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, both AttributeError on cv2.dnn; missing DictValue vs broken install. <b>unrelated</b>, Issue A is a genuine bug where cv2.dnn.DictValue is missing during import in OpenCV 4.8+, while Issue B was a user typo (readNetFromCAFFE vs readNetFromCaffe) in OpenCV 3.2.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/25744">#25744</a> error: (-216:No CUDA support) OpenCV was not built to work with the selected device. Please check CUDA_ARCH_PTX or CUDA_ARCH_BIN in your build configuration. in function &#x27;initCUDABackend&#x27;a support </td><td>like #18382, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both issues are the same &#x27;No CUDA support&#x27; error caused by incorrect CUDA_ARCH_BIN/PTX settings not matching the GPU&#x27;s compute capability.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/26674">#26674</a> Please upload ONNX model to reproduce error</td><td>like #18209, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve Reshape layer assertion failures in computeShapeByReshapeMask but with different assertion conditions (maskTotal vs dstTotal).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/27885">#27885</a> deviating results of findChessboardCorners</td><td>like #15712, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve chessboard corner detection in calib3d, but A concerns regression in findChessboardCorners (regular) while B concerns findChessboardCornersSB (SB variant) with different symptoms and root causes.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29692">#29692</a> cv2.fisheye.calibrate() fails with (N, 1, C) point arrays in OpenCV 5.0.0</td><td>like #9150, closed as completed (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both involve cv2.fisheye.calibrate() array shape handling, but B was a 3.2 binding issue requiring (N,1,3) shape, while A is a 5.0 core regression rejecting that previously-working shape due to 0d/1d Mat changes.</td></tr>
</table></details>

<details><summary><h2 style="display:inline;border:none;padding:0;margin:0">Open issues that a later merged PR seems to fix (64)</h2></summary>
<table class="rep" style="table-layout:fixed;width:100%"><colgroup><col width="30%" style="width:30%"><col width="20%" style="width:20%"><col width="50%" style="width:50%"></colgroup>
<tr><th width="30%">Item</th><th width="20%">Proposal (confidence)</th><th width="50%">Check</th></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/27452">#27452</a> Enable V4L2 Stateless HEVC Hardware Acceleration for Raspberry Pi 5</td><td>merged PR #27453 is its nearest match and has no link to it (0.96)</td><td><b>partly</b>, PR implements HEVC decoding for RPi5 but has unresolved CMake (HAVE_DRM) and runtime capture failures, plus missing tests/docs.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29150">#29150</a> [Bug] UBSan: load of invalid enum value in SunRasterDecoder::readHeader() (grfmt_sunras.cpp:72)</td><td>merged PR #29151 is its nearest match and has no link to it (0.96)</td><td><b>fixed</b>, PR #29151 adds validation of raw maptype and ras_type values before casting to enum, exactly addressing the UBSan load-invalid-value UB described in issue #29150, and includes tests for the reported crash case.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/19648">#19648</a> Temp file creation on Windows name conflict/race condition</td><td>merged PR #28087 is its nearest match and has no link to it (0.94)</td><td><b>fixed</b>, PR B directly addresses issue #19648 by replacing GetTempFileNameA with GUID-based naming using CoCreateGuid, eliminating the race condition as proposed in the issue.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/30569">#30569</a> cv::reduce supports only 5 of 13 depths (CV_32S, CV_8S and all newer depths unsupported)</td><td>merged PR #30570 is its nearest match and has no link to it (0.94)</td><td><b>partly</b>, PR adds support for CV_8S and CV_32S but leaves 6 other unsupported depths (CV_16F, CV_16BF, CV_Bool, CV_64U, CV_64S, CV_32U) for a follow-up.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/14461">#14461</a> Positional Argument `outImg` Should Be Keyword Argument in cv2.drawMatches</td><td>merged PR #30509 is its nearest match and has no link to it (0.93)</td><td><b>related</b>, The PR adds documentation about passing None for outImg but does not change the API to make it a keyword argument as requested in the issue.</td></tr>

<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29863">#29863</a> imgproc: add validation mask for ill-conditioned warp perspective pixels</td><td>#29873 (Improve warpPerspective and remap documentation regarding interpolati…) also closes #29816: keep one (0.99)</td><td><b>overlap</b>, Both PRs fix the same issue (#29816) by modifying Imgproc_WarpPerspective_Test.accuracy, but PR A adds a validation mask to exclude ill-conditioned pixels while PR B raises tolerance and documents quantization behavior.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29980">#29980</a> doc: correct camera matrix order in initInverseRectificationMap description</td><td>#29992 (test: pin cameraMatrix/newCameraMatrix order in initInverseRectificationMap) also closes #22262: keep one (0.99)</td><td><b>related</b>, PR A fixes documentation for initInverseRectificationMap matrix order, PR B adds a regression test for the same issue (#22262); they are companion changes addressing the same bug from different angles.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/30377">#30377</a> photo: weight the CalibrateDebevec gauge equation like the data equations 🤖🤖🤖</td><td>#30378 (photo: weight the CalibrateDebevec gauge equation like the data equations (5.x) 🤖🤖🤖) also closes #30376: keep one (0.99)</td><td><b>related</b>, PR #30378 is explicitly a port of PR #30377 to the 5.x branch, making them sibling fixes for different release lines.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29285">#29285</a> [ROCm] Add AMD GPU support for cv::cuda via HIP (core)</td><td>looks like #29527 ([ROCm/HIP] Add AMD GPU support for cv::cuda via HIP (5.x port)) by another author (0.96)</td><td><b>related</b>, PR B is a port of PR A to the OpenCV 5.x branch; they implement the same HIP/ROCm backend for cv::cuda but target different release branches (4.x vs 5.x).</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/27155">#27155</a> [WIP] modules/videoio: add libcamera backend for OpenCV Video I/O</td><td>looks like #27554 (modules/videoio: add libcamera backend for OpenCV VideoCapture) by another author (0.94)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both PRs implement a libcamera backend for OpenCV VideoCapture with nearly identical scope and purpose; they are competing implementations for the same feature.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/28942">#28942</a> ml(DTrees): fix crash when training with default CVFolds=10</td><td>looks like #29136 (fix: prevent DTrees::train crash by changing CVFolds default from 10 to 0) by another author (0.94)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both PRs fix the identical DTrees crash by changing the default CVFolds from 10 to 0 in TreeParams, addressing the same root cause with the same solution.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/29746">#29746</a> video: make BackgroundSubtractorKNN honor zero learning rate 🤖🤖🤖</td><td>looks like #30179 (video: freeze BackgroundSubtractorKNN model for a zero learning rate) by another author (0.94)</td><td><b>same</b>, Both PRs fix the identical bug in BackgroundSubtractorKNN where zero learning rate was ignored, address the same issue #24226, implement the same freeze logic for CPU and OpenCL paths, and add equivalent regression tests.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/30788">#30788</a> calib3d: use P3P as the solvePnPRansac kernel for planar objects</td><td>looks like #30880 (calib3d: keep the best of EPnP and P3P for exactly 4 points in solvePnP) by another author (0.93)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both PRs address EPnP/P3P issues in calib3d but target different functions: A fixes solvePnPRansac for planar objects, B fixes solvePnP for exactly 4 points.</td></tr>
<tr><td><a href="https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/30665">#30665</a> core: compute multiply and divide in CV_64F when the result is CV_64F</td><td>looks like #30767 (core: compute 64-bit integer multiply and divide exactly 🤖🤖🤖) by another author (0.91)</td><td><b>related</b>, Both PRs fix precision issues in cv::multiply and cv::divide but for different type combinations: #30665 ensures CV_64F output is computed in 64-bit float, while #30767 adds exact 64-bit integer kernels for CV_64U/CV_64S inputs and outputs; the author of #30767 explicitly states it does not overlap with #30665.</td></tr>
</table></details>
